<script> 
window.addEventListener('load', function() {
var $searchBox = $('#main-search'),
formSearch = document.getElementById('site-search'),
placeholderText = $searchBox.attr('placeholder'),
$CC_StoreInformation = $(".CC_StoreInformation"),
$CC_StoreDetails = $(".CC_StoreDetails");
if(((!Site.RWD.isMqMatching(['M','S']))
&& ($searchBox.val().length != 0))
|| ((!Site.RWD.isMqMatching(['M','S']))
&& (($(window).scrollTop() < 100)
&& ($searchBox.val().length === 0))
&& ((!$CC_StoreInformation.length) && (!$CC_StoreDetails.length)))) {
$searchBox.focus();
}
formSearch.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
var searchObject = JSON.stringify({ 'term' : $('input[name=SearchTerm]').val(), 'type' : $('input[name=searchResultTab]').val()});
window.localStorage.setItem('searchLayer', searchObject);
});
$searchBox.on('focusin', function() { 
if(!$searchBox.val().length){
$searchBox.attr('placeholder','');
}
})
.on('focusout', function() { 
$searchBox.attr('placeholder', placeholderText);
});
// Gets the search term from window.location.search and sets it in the search input field.
function setSearchTerm(term) {
var reservedChars = {
';' : '%3B', 
',' : '%2C', 
'/' : '%2F', 
'?' : '%3F', 
':' : '%3A', 
'@' : '%40', 
'&' : '%26', 
'=' : '%3D', 
'+' : '%2B', 
'$' : '%24', 
"#" : '%23' };
var searchTerm = decodeURI(window.location.search.replace(new RegExp("^(?:.*[&?]" + encodeURI(term) + "(?:=([^&]*))?)?.*$", "i"), "$1"));
searchTerm = searchTerm.replace(/\+/g, " ");
if(searchTerm.length) { 
for(key in reservedChars) { 
searchTerm = searchTerm.replace(new RegExp(reservedChars[key],'g'), key); 
}
}
if(!$('#main-search').val().length){
$('#main-search').val(searchTerm);
}
} 
setSearchTerm('SearchTerm');
}); 
</script>

}

}

function getCookie(name) {
var value = "; " + document.cookie;
var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
return (parts.length == 2) ? parts.pop().split(";").shift() : '';
}
function getMyCity() {
var cookie = getCookie('sessionStatus');
if (cookie != '') {
var myCity = cookie.split('|');
if (myCity.length > 1 && myCity[1] != '') {
return decodeURIComponent(myCity[1]);
}
}
return '';
} 
function getMyStore() {
var cookie = getCookie('mystore');
if (cookie == '') {
return '';
}
else {
var cookieObject = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(cookie).replace(/\+/g, ' '));
return cookieObject.name;
}
}
